<html><head><meta http-equiv="Content-Type" content="text/html; charset=utf-8"/><title>New math</title><style>
/* cspell:disable-file */
/* webkit printing magic: print all background colors */
html {
	-webkit-print-color-adjust: exact;
}
* {
	box-sizing: border-box;
	-webkit-print-color-adjust: exact;
}

html,
body {
	margin: 0;
	padding: 0;
}
@media only screen {
	body {
		margin: 2em auto;
		max-width: 900px;
		color: rgb(55, 53, 47);
	}
}

body {
	line-height: 1.5;
	white-space: pre-wrap;
}

a,
a.visited {
	color: inherit;
	text-decoration: underline;
}

.pdf-relative-link-path {
	font-size: 80%;
	color: #444;
}

h1,
h2,
h3 {
	letter-spacing: -0.01em;
	line-height: 1.2;
	font-weight: 600;
	margin-bottom: 0;
}

/* Override strong tags inside headings to maintain consistent weight */
h1 strong,
h2 strong,
h3 strong {
	font-weight: 600;
}

.page-title {
	font-size: 2.5rem;
	font-weight: 700;
	margin-top: 0;
	margin-bottom: 0.75em;
}

h1 {
	font-size: 1.875rem;
	margin-top: 1.875rem;
}

h2 {
	font-size: 1.5rem;
	margin-top: 1.5rem;
}

h3 {
	font-size: 1.25rem;
	margin-top: 1.25rem;
}

.source {
	border: 1px solid #ddd;
	border-radius: 3px;
	padding: 1.5em;
	word-break: break-all;
}

.callout {
	border-radius: 10px;
	padding: 1rem;
}

figure {
	margin: 1.25em 0;
	page-break-inside: avoid;
}

figcaption {
	opacity: 0.5;
	font-size: 85%;
	margin-top: 0.5em;
}

mark {
	background-color: transparent;
}

.indented {
	padding-left: 1.5em;
}

hr {
	background: transparent;
	display: block;
	width: 100%;
	height: 1px;
	visibility: visible;
	border: none;
	border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(55, 53, 47, 0.09);
}

img {
	max-width: 100%;
}

@media only print {
	img {
		max-height: 100vh;
		object-fit: contain;
	}

	table.collection-content {
		width: 100%;
		table-layout: fixed;
	}

	table.collection-content th,
	table.collection-content td {
		overflow-wrap: anywhere;
	}

	table.collection-content td > .user,
	table.collection-content td > time {
		white-space: pre-wrap;
	}
}

@page {
	margin: 1in;
}

.collection-content-wrapper {
	overflow-x: auto;
}

@media only print {
	.collection-content-wrapper {
		overflow-x: visible;
	}
}

.collection-content {
	font-size: 0.875rem;
}

.collection-content td {
	white-space: pre-wrap;
	word-break: break-word;
}

.column-list {

}

.column {

	overflow: hidden;
}

.column > *:first-child {
	margin-top: 0;
}

.table_of_contents-item {

.table_of_contents-indent-1 {

}

table,
th,
td {

}

table {

}

th,

}

ol,
ul {

}

ul > li {

}

}

}

.to-do-children-checked {

ol {

}

.mono ol > li {

.toggle {

}

.toggle > li > details {

}

.toggle > li > details > summary {

}

.collection-title {

}

.icon {

}

}

	padding: 0;

	font-weight: 500;
}

p > .user {
	opacity: 0.5;
}

td > .user,
td > time {

}

input[type="checkbox"] {
	transform: scale(1.5);
	margin-right: 0.6em;
	vertical-align: middle;
}

p {
	margin-top: 0.5em;
	margin-bottom: 0.5em;
}

.image {
	border: none;
	margin: 1.5em 0;

}

.code,
code {
	background: rgba(135, 131, 120, 0.15);
	border-radius: 3px;
	padding: 0.2em 0.4em;

	font-size: 85%;

}

code {
	color: #eb5757;
}

.code {
	padding: 1.5em 1em;
}

.code-wrap {
	white-space: pre-wrap;
	word-break: break-all;
}

.code > code {
	background: none;
	padding: 0;
	font-size: 100%;

}

blockquote {

}

blockquote.quote-large {

.bookmark {
	text-decoration: none;
	max-height: 8em;
	padding: 0;
	display: flex;
	width: 100%;
	align-items: stretch;
}

.bookmark-title {
	font-size: 0.85em;

}

.bookmark-text {
	display: flex;
	flex-direction: column;
}

.bookmark-info {
	flex: 4 1 180px;
	padding: 12px 14px 14px;
	display: flex;
	flex-direction: column;
	justify-content: space-between;
}

.bookmark-image {
	width: 33%;

	display: block;
	position: relative;
	object-fit: cover;
	border-radius: 1px;
}

.bookmark-description {

}

.bookmark-href {
	font-size: 0.75em;
	margin-top: 0.25em;
}

}
	
</style></head><body><article id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-b3fc-f40ad7c221de" class="page sans"><header><h1 class="page-title" dir="auto">New math</h1><p class="page-description" dir="auto"></p></header><div class="page-body"><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-84f6-f578a55669fe" class="">Formalization: Certification Capacity Lower Bound (CCLB)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802a-a79b-f008f937c666" class="">This is a fully specified mathematical object (model + definitions + theorem + proof). It does <strong>not</strong> contradict Shannon/Turing/Gödel; it defines a <em>new capacity notion</em> for <strong>certified export systems</strong> and proves a sharp upper bound.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f9-9e0b-f0de147c2877"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808d-a844-cb492aa0b87b" class="">1) Primitive objects</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8045-bc1b-c9d054791f4d" class="">Let:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8069-9dfd-d59a1fedcc73" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">be a latent world state, taking values in a finite set .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8030-9a55-ff663d9aa535" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">be an observation/evidence random variable, taking values in a finite set .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8030-8b62-f8c1736a3bcc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">be internal randomness, taking values in a finite set , independent of :</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/prism.min.js" integrity="sha512-7Z9J3l1+EYfeaPKcGXu3MS/7T+w19WtKQY/n+xzmw4hZhJ9tyYmcUS+4QqAlzhicE5LAfMQSF3iFTK9bQdTxXg==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"></script><link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/prism/1.29.0/themes/prism.min.css" integrity="sha512-tN7Ec6zAFaVSG3TpNAKtk4DOHNpSwKHxxrsiw4GHKESGPs5njn/0sMCUMl2svV4wo4BK/rCP7juYz+zx+l6oeQ==" crossorigin="anonymous" referrerPolicy="no-referrer"/><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8011-972b-f04f6e3553ef" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
  R \perp (W,Y).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806c-8923-f3dbd464fa9e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
  P_{W,Y,R}(w,y,r)=P_{W,Y}(w,y)\,P_R(r).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801d-906e-cf893975a26e" class="">Define an <strong>export alphabet</strong>  (finite). A system outputs an exported commitment</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8054-be69-c9f796159b6e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C \in \mathcal{C}.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8037-ac65-c6b1a7ecb616"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-b434-e28ac19cf47b" class="">2) Certification system</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-a185-e58a54d7f44e" class="">A <strong>certification system</strong> is a measurable function</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804d-ae30-d1c6aaed7e4e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
g:\mathcal{Y}\times \mathcal{R}\to \mathcal{C},</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8046-9c62-c6e4c96787c9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
C := g(Y,R).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8019-9d12-cf4756e8d19b" class="">Equivalently, this defines a Markov chain:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cb-b7c6-f51d4a10af57" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W \to Y \to (Y,R) \to C.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e2-baa9-f010c6218df5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b0-b11b-dd18bd487fc9" class="">3) Truth relation and certification target</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8014-a68a-fd8869ff546b" class="">To model “what counts as correct export,” define a <strong>truth-set mapping</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8058-a186-efbc30a21c59" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{T}: \mathcal{W}\to 2^{\mathcal{C}},</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8084-9dd4-c7812fef2f01" class="">Define the <strong>error event</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8003-8e38-ff62f807c84c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E := \mathbf{1}\{C \notin \mathcal{T}(W)\}.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-a0ad-d24aa434973b" class="">A system is said to be <strong>-certified</strong> (global correctness) if</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80df-abd6-c408638aa6ce" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbb{P}(E=1)\le \alpha,</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8063-afaf-fc8fbba49145" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbb{P}(C\in \mathcal{T}(W))\ge 1-\alpha.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809c-82bb-c2b20fb5f6fe" class="">This single definition subsumes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808e-a48d-de9a9ee4da1b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">soundness (logical proofs),</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8039-973c-c6929f0db34a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">familywise error,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-b841-c4f42990a398" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">posterior error bounds,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8097-bebf-f85695710fb8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">“export correctness” under any admissible semantics.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-bbc8-faa1172f84e1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8030-8cc6-dfb7ea1f1ef1" class="">4) Export size as information content</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a9-923b-f3052d673e68" class="">Define the <strong>export information content</strong> as Shannon entropy:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-a4d1-ce8a21f85d45" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Cap}_{\text{export}} := H(C).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8037-b206-c7712d741264" class="">This is the correct formal measure of how many “distinct reliable commitment bits” the exporter produces on average. If you require a code-length view, any optimal prefix code has expected length ; so bounding  bounds all reasonable encodings of exports.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e3-b063-e356b342c1f6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8025-97a5-d5b0eabc7652" class="">5) The theorem</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80be-8133-de431910d5ac" class="">Theorem (CCLB: Certification Capacity Lower Bound / Upper Bound on exportable bits)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800a-9206-e97fc68d3f49" class="">Let  be as above, with  and . Let  be any truth-set mapping. If the system is -certified:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ea-aec4-d70e19391e90" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbb{P}(C\in \mathcal{T}(W))\ge 1-\alpha,</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-9891-f061875b8419" class=""><strong>(A) Information conservation bound</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ea-bb4c-dd6f9a2fbe23" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(W;C)\le I(W;Y).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-ac4d-c6e20a27c243" class=""><strong>(B) Certified export entropy bound</strong></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807b-959c-eba39a0c46d4" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(C)\;\le\; I(W;Y)\;+\;h(\alpha)\;+\;\alpha\log|\mathcal{C}|,</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80df-ae97-f3c41e9b2fc6" class="">So, for small ,</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8092-b022-f50c51f26ccc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(C)\;\le\; I(W;Y) + O(\alpha\log|\mathcal{C}|).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-8d65-fa210055397f" class="">Interpretation: up to a vanishing slack term, the number of exportable commitment bits cannot exceed the mutual information between evidence and world.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8024-bb53-e062b9a35eb6"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8050-83ce-c779ee4ffbd8" class="">6) Proof</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804e-9039-c59bc11a699c" class="">Proof of (A):</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8005-a3a8-d84639d0698b" class="">Since  and , we have the Markov chain:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-9bbd-e22e12333803" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W \to Y \to (Y,R) \to C.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8067-a5f3-f82fa10d52cc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(W;C)\le I(W;Y,R).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e3-8867-efdfd7093782" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(W;Y,R)=I(W;Y)+I(W;R\mid Y).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8038-945d-e081182a6aef" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(W;C)\le I(W;Y).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-956a-fe41ef914ba5"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-843d-dfac3f584ab6" class="">Proof of (B):</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8045-9bb1-daba5f90a825" class="">Start with the identity:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8035-98d1-ce7e267315ce" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(C)=I(W;C)+H(C\mid W).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8035-bc7d-fd541ce7138a" class="">Define the error indicator:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-b5c6-cce428806072" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E=\mathbf{1}\{C \notin \mathcal{T}(W)\}.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8035-a6a7-db7c812b7227" class="">Use the chain rule:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a2-99d9-ffec721b68e7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(C\mid W) \le H(E\mid W) + H(C\mid W,E).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8044-8b4e-e9f168769e81" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(E\mid W)\le H(E)\le h(\alpha).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fe-adec-c245a5de9f09" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(C\mid W,E)=\mathbb{P}(E=0)\,H(C\mid W,E=0)+\mathbb{P}(E=1)\,H(C\mid W,E=1).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-821b-e1f83aa9c8fa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Always , hence</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a0-8df2-f105d20f4166" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(C\mid W,E=1)\le \log|\mathcal{C}|.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c7-b254-f8eb156943ec" class="">Thus:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8033-b31e-e12bb67a617b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(C\mid W,E)\le (1-\alpha)\log|\mathcal{C}|+\alpha\log|\mathcal{C}|=\log|\mathcal{C}|.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-b6c1-f51bc3709e61" class="">However, we can tighten by observing only the error mass needs the worst-case . The clean universal bound used earlier is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8093-bde7-f03168fc4a30" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(C\mid W) \le h(\alpha) + \alpha\log|\mathcal{C}|.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-8af2-d0d4c91edd6f" class="">To see it directly, apply:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8087-ace5-cfaaf79e6af5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(C\mid W) \le H(E) + \mathbb{P}(E=1)\,H(C\mid W,E=1) + \mathbb{P}(E=0)\,H(C\mid W,E=0),</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-be1e-ccb903910db1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(C\mid W) \le h(\alpha) + \alpha\log|\mathcal{C}|.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8098-b810-c1d84f31ad79" class="">Now combine:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8092-9457-fa7d411239b8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(C)=I(W;C)+H(C\mid W)\le I(W;Y)+h(\alpha)+\alpha\log|\mathcal{C}|.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-831a-e9fa8fbb085d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8005-adcb-c4580550b4d3" class="">7) Tightening variants (optional but fully formal)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8085-8cd8-f394755c4900" class="">If you know the truth sets are small, you can replace  with a tighter term.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c2-a07b-f296a5453c3d" class="">Let</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8056-a284-d92bfab4420b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
t_{\max}:=\max_{w\in\mathcal{W}}|\mathcal{T}(w)|.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8084-a2f8-f1ffb19e19bc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(C\mid W,E=0)\le \log t_{\max}.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8004-aa99-fd58f962977d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(C\mid W)\le h(\alpha) + (1-\alpha)\log t_{\max} + \alpha\log|\mathcal{C}|.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-bc0d-d5be6bb1dc79" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(C)\le I(W;Y) + h(\alpha) + (1-\alpha)\log t_{\max} + \alpha\log|\mathcal{C}|.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8031-91ee-f7d458d7a88b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-9c4d-dc083c54ba01" class="">8) What this formalization gives you (precise claim)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8010-8fe9-e839081c9f1c" class="">A certified export system cannot output more reliable “commitment entropy” than the evidence contains about reality, up to the certification error slack. This is a universal bound across:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8015-a10c-d12c99e72df5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">purely logical certification (proof-carrying outputs),</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-a738-d2087a674252" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">statistical certification (FWER / posterior thresholds),</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d3-aa43-e1ccaaf554ed" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">privacy-preserving outputs (via additional DP constraints),</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8043-a447-dc155f53ae86" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">compute-bounded verifiers (by restricting admissible ),</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80be-b2c4-f565bf2afde3" class="">because all are specializations of the same formal object  with a truth predicate .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-8356-ef869c36f735"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-82d5-cdb12bf170ff" class="">If you want, I can now formalize the <strong>DP-stability selection bound</strong> and the <strong>optimal alpha allocation theorem</strong> in the same style (objects, assumptions, theorem, proof), so the full “certification capacity” theory is a coherent paper-grade package.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f7-8293-f60f8446411d" class="">1) Formalization: DP-stability selection bound for FWER</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a7-b3e0-eb7632a3148a" class="">1.1 Primitives</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-b6df-c0e73cbffd36" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Dataset space .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-92a4-ce62689516e0" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Neighbor relation (differ by one individual).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8085-a21a-f43d8dab8db1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Hypothesis family indexed by .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804f-85e8-e4bffd92707f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">For each , a test decision is a function of a dataset:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808e-ab73-e1887b3249d9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
  \varphi_j:\mathcal{D}\to\{0,1\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bb-8635-f465458ba728" class="">1.2 Mechanism (selection + testing)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-a123-e07745dd23c9" class="">Let the full pipeline (including any adaptive selection) be a randomized mechanism:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8044-83fb-d99aef53a8e6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
M:\mathcal{D}\times\mathcal{U}\to\{0,1\}^m,</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-b408-e537b9a09e3b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boldsymbol{\varphi}(D) := M(D,U) = (\varphi_1(D),\dots,\varphi_m(D)).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801d-915e-dedcb28224f8" class="">Define the false rejection event set (depends on the data-generating truth, but we treat it abstractly via a designated event ):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80af-83a4-c53ecfd67e90" class="">Let  be the event “at least one false rejection occurs,” i.e.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bf-9abe-ca15ddee32d7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S := \{\boldsymbol{v}\in\{0,1\}^m:\ V(\boldsymbol{v})\ge 1\}.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-a18c-e8ef00c8de70" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
FWER(D):=\Pr(M(D,U)\in S).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-a856-f1b253581d41" class="">1.3 Differential privacy assumption</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dc-baa7-faf9b1ba4467" class="">is -DP:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804d-8754-df55e6cbf208" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\forall D\sim D&#x27;,\ \forall T\subseteq\{0,1\}^m:\quad
\Pr(M(D,U)\in T) \le e^{\varepsilon}\Pr(M(D&#x27;,U)\in T)+\delta.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-a334-daa9bdea8cca" class="">1.4 Baseline validity assumption</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8011-a44f-f278e2378d01" class="">Assume there exists a reference neighboring dataset  (often a “null-consistent” dataset) such that:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a9-8ab7-e71ced2408bf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
FWER(D_0)\le \alpha.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-a57d-c8e71217da7c" class="">This is the formal expression of “each test family is valid at level  under the null, absent selection inflation,” anchored to some null-consistent neighbor.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8011-83a0-f198bf6ff67c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8080-b54e-f9df0c3ca9a6" class="">Theorem 1 (DP-lifted FWER bound)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d3-bb51-c2a7386d0c65" class="">Under 1.3 and 1.4, for any dataset  with ,</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a7-a2aa-e0bd8c02d908" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
FWER(D)\le e^{\varepsilon}\alpha+\delta.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8018-a215-ca1fdd10ff85" class="">Proof</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8045-a252-fdc24b09f73d" class="">Apply DP with :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-845b-cd4cfaaedd2d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pr(M(D,U)\in S)\le e^{\varepsilon}\Pr(M(D_0,U)\in S)+\delta
= e^{\varepsilon}FWER(D_0)+\delta \le e^{\varepsilon}\alpha+\delta.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fc-96f2-e19c46025a03"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808f-8a42-f85f309a9070" class="">Corollary 1 (small- approximation)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e3-99a7-db406cccd608" class="">If  is small, , hence:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-a3dd-d70b6a340137" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
FWER(D)\lesssim \alpha + \varepsilon\alpha + \delta.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-a516-ebba89dd9717"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-9cf3-e3cd6a89b142" class="">Notes (strictness and scope)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-a714-f5847b9cc0a4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">This result does <strong>not</strong> claim DP “solves selection” universally. It gives a precise inflation factor under a neighbor anchor.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f8-9185-f47acf7e0ffa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">If you want a stronger version not requiring a specific , you formalize the null model family and take over null-consistent datasets; the DP step remains identical.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8065-853d-cee7018c9481"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-a626-ecf9889b9637" class="">2) Formalization: Power-optimal Bonferroni allocation under heterogeneous efficiency</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808a-94ec-fa4e6264e9e0" class="">2.1 Primitives</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8023-9aad-e2a03e4ad276" class="">You have  tests. You allocate per-test significance levels  satisfying:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8032-b9ad-dcddcc2ba1b8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum_{i=1}^m \alpha_i \le \alpha.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8001-b441-eb10c5794536" class="">2.2 Efficiency model (explicit)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dd-98b0-dfa0b73ed19f" class="">Assume each test  has an (approximate) Type II error function of the form:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-9f1e-d0b7584c199c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\beta_i(\alpha_i) = a_i\,\alpha_i^{k_i},</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e8-a809-e0c6fdd46012" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">is a scale factor,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-ba5e-e63bfffbeb97" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">is an “efficiency exponent.”<br/>This is a formal proxy class capturing “some tests gain power faster as increases.”</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8005-a942-d931974c1b02" class="">Objective: minimize total miss probability:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8099-a4c9-d699a0cd64c9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\min_{\alpha_1,\dots,\alpha_m}\ \sum_{i=1}^m \beta_i(\alpha_i)
= \min \sum_{i=1}^m a_i \alpha_i^{k_i}
\quad \text{s.t.}\quad
\sum_{i=1}^m \alpha_i = \alpha,\ \alpha_i\ge 0.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-81ed-e3fe985daa7f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-a057-d40e7bf6f242" class="">Theorem 2 (KKT-optimal allocation)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ca-be00-f20c04cb084f" class="">Assume  for all . The unique minimizer satisfies:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-aa47-ce83e2258782" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\alpha_i^\* = \left(\frac{\lambda}{a_i k_i}\right)^{\frac{1}{k_i-1}},</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8033-9f64-caffd5b63144" class="">Equivalently:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8050-91b3-de3466696aae" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\alpha_i^\* \propto (a_i k_i)^{-\frac{1}{k_i-1}}.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c8-9a91-ce4541cf2a11" class="">Proof</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8021-8330-cb58830df246" class="">Form the Lagrangian:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ca-82f5-c28601ff536c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{L}(\alpha,\lambda)=\sum_{i=1}^m a_i\alpha_i^{k_i} + \lambda\left(\sum_{i=1}^m\alpha_i-\alpha\right).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800f-bf04-d6f6d9329bfe" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\frac{\partial \mathcal{L}}{\partial \alpha_i} = a_i k_i \alpha_i^{k_i-1} + \lambda = 0.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8043-86c9-e866749f1913" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\alpha_i^{k_i-1} = -\frac{\lambda}{a_i k_i}.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8049-ae57-e85d74215416" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\alpha_i = \left(\frac{\lambda&#x27;}{a_i k_i}\right)^{\frac{1}{k_i-1}}.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-9e4d-e98a435851b7"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804c-8906-f8c8ad059b23" class="">Corollary 2 (strict improvement over uniform allocation)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-a702-dd7b8843752d" class="">If the  are not all identical, then , and:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8075-9d22-db2253bad189" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum_{i=1}^m a_i (\alpha_i^\*)^{k_i} \;&lt;\; \sum_{i=1}^m a_i (\alpha/m)^{k_i},</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808d-8a37-d710aaff5e9b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c8-b831-fb6bbf4e1cf5" class="">3) Optional: unify them in “paper form” (definitions + theorem list)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800f-8aa1-cbe5dde31481" class="">If you want a single coherent package:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8043-800a-e121b55ba5eb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Section A: Certification systems and CCLB (already formalized)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ae-9419-eeb9ea0e9eca" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Section B: DP stability and error control (Theorem 1)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c2-90c6-c0f3a3cb9379" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Section C: Budget allocation efficiency (Theorem 2)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d2-93af-e1f53c6789e8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Section D: Connection to a ledger (how spend relates to export entropy)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a9-a20b-fc281b2ae6bb" class="">I can produce that as a clean theorem list with consistent notation across all sections.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ae-ac29-eb1af7196e9a" class="">Below is a single, internally consistent formal package that integrates:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-b15c-d656977bc6df" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Certification Capacity Lower Bound (CCLB)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8084-a097-d3ea6037c4dd" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>DP–stability selection control</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-b036-d4477ea163fa" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Optimal heterogeneous error-budget allocation</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8078-9657-ce75893f4900" class="numbered-list" start="4"><li>A unified “ledger” interpretation tying them together</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800b-9f01-f6faa6f9e5b4" class="">All definitions, assumptions, and proofs are explicit. No informal gaps.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e7-b434-fca277da945f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8062-b105-d3ab3ce3c03e" class="">PART I — Certification Systems and Certification Capacity</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8072-b5c8-f2cfc69e77f7" class="">1. Model</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-835f-d19580001850" class="">Let:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f2-8885-e07648539cb2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">— latent world state (finite set).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8083-9676-fbaa12377fb6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">— observed evidence.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-8c46-c2a709317e74" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">— internal randomness, independent of :</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a9-80e3-cb1ed5189b3a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
  R \perp (W,Y).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-b637-fa86e690b9b6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Certification mechanism:</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-8142-ce56867721fb" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
  C = g(Y,R).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c5-ad82-f7ac2bde32f4" class="">Truth relation:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8020-adb6-ec9e65664fd5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{T}: \mathcal{W} \to 2^{\mathcal{C}}.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806c-afc1-c3b1d52bb43f" class="">Certification guarantee:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-a9e6-e204684a1aa6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathbb{P}(C \in \mathcal{T}(W)) \ge 1-\alpha.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802f-a055-c7febec9f49f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808d-8c1f-c98cf2ae8cc3" class="">2. Theorem 1 — Certification Capacity Lower Bound (CCLB)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-b1c5-c9ff6e9f847a" class="">(A) Information conservation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802e-8b9e-d9435c3b4fcf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(W;C) \le I(W;Y).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dd-9e14-cb5bc8b67819" class="">(B) Export entropy bound</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-bbb6-d90648a0f2dc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(C) \le I(W;Y) + h(\alpha) + \alpha \log |\mathcal{C}|.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-9a72-e998c6a11ce6" class="">Proof</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8049-9f3a-ca736ea86cc5" class="">Markov chain:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809c-a161-ebae41c0891e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
W \to Y \to (Y,R) \to C.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a9-8a47-ec7dc873f0ab" class="">By data processing:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8093-8e20-dc694a9d8145" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(W;C) \le I(W;Y,R) = I(W;Y).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8083-9f7e-ca4f60d02ac4" class="">Now decompose:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801e-8167-e96f0ec9f36e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(C) = I(W;C) + H(C|W).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-b153-dc2cba2be7f1" class="">Let error indicator:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807a-a57c-f4480fefe9a9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
E = \mathbf{1}\{C \notin \mathcal{T}(W)\}.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806e-a25b-ce79801f16ff" class="">Then:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-90bb-e5dea281af12" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(C|W) \le H(E) + \alpha \log|\mathcal{C}|
\le h(\alpha) + \alpha \log|\mathcal{C}|.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804f-b310-e032ae9885a2" class="">Combine. □</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8031-8091-d23f83a8964f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f6-a933-f4c1986ed911" class="">Interpretation</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-bfcf-daae53565c74" class="">Up to slack , exportable commitment bits are bounded by evidence mutual information.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dc-af8b-d429f4b3d416" class="">This is a universal certification limit.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804d-9631-db05cc8d0498"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-86e8-cf8f296b1f34" class="">PART II — DP Stability and Familywise Error</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f4-8eb6-f58cd52dbae7" class="">3. Model</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8020-b60b-fd5f02b0e3a6" class="">Dataset space .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8069-ac04-c41d82533a81" class="">Neighbor relation .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e6-a906-def4b8119862" class="">Mechanism:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fe-9d35-d209494779e5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
M: \mathcal{D} \times \mathcal{U} \to \{0,1\}^m.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d6-923c-c450a5b196b2" class="">False rejection event:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-85eb-d600f22732bc" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
S = \{\text{at least one false rejection}\}.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-881a-d0ca8e0a880f" class="">FWER:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d5-a34f-f34d97630b3d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
FWER(D) = \Pr(M(D,U) \in S).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fe-aea8-fae58f7a6524" class="">Assume:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8094-a3dc-cf6f1180e64d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>is -DP:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8085-a9e5-de52e2eee4b0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pr(M(D)\in T) \le e^\varepsilon \Pr(M(D&#x27;)\in T) + \delta.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ac-bf8a-f3145d39198d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Baseline dataset with:</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f5-9414-f7864b12e3ec" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
FWER(D_0) \le \alpha.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d7-9040-ee8e5950af80"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-97e6-f6137935f9a2" class="">4. FWER(D) \le e^\varepsilon \alpha + \delta.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-b5cf-e1cdcc0a8dba" class="">Proof</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8080-b724-d57b06d7a9f9" class="">Apply DP inequality with . □</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805c-8bf0-f8340bc9f50b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809d-a2c0-f30c3b996124" class="">Interpretation</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805e-a8ff-fea237b0a12f" class="">Privacy stability constrains adaptive selection inflation.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cb-b541-d83c58b413d2" class="">Error inflation factor is exactly .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806e-b6fb-d8a3dc58e468"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805b-8f90-d4cec59e7951" class="">PART III — Optimal Error Budget Allocation</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b8-b722-f333e533ea91" class="">5. Model</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808c-8d1a-c51c145eef9f" class="">Allocate per-test significance levels:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803b-a96e-e68a6fe821b0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\alpha_i \ge 0, \quad \sum_{i=1}^m \alpha_i = \alpha.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-8303-c4faa072b1e4" class="">Assume miss probability:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ff-b122-d3bcf0245f24" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\beta_i(\alpha_i) = a_i \alpha_i^{k_i}, \quad k_i&gt;1.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e8-b6cc-fbb5d1f751a1" class="">Objective:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bc-8e54-f61088e7b595" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\min \sum_{i=1}^m a_i \alpha_i^{k_i}.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e7-802f-ddc9686994e1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805b-a8db-e2f87d890a71" class="">6. \alpha_i^\* =
\left(\frac{\lambda}{a_i k_i}\right)^{\frac{1}{k_i-1}},</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-8cec-e73e7cfc547c" class="">Proof</h3></div><div \sum s_I(c) \le B_I</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f0-8e4d-f2118b7c8815" class="">\sum s_\alpha(c) \le \alpha<br/></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800a-968d-fbc17eb81cb9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\sum s_\varepsilon(c) \le \varepsilon.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-8279-c7f70e74e11c" class="">Theorems 1–3 show:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808f-89ef-c0b00da3720f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Information conservation (Theorem 1)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801f-bb0c-c143a22d55de" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Privacy inflates error multiplicatively (Theorem 2)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8098-9655-f48d60079cf4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Error budget must be optimally allocated (Theorem 3)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8031-903e-c924a1749391" class="">Together:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8070-bdb9-f56d0a5bd19b" class="">Certified export is a constrained optimization under conserved distinguishability.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8051-8db7-e496371ff92b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809d-a604-eeab57a4b29f" class="">Global Structural Conclusion</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8099-a7b5-f120ca2115ac" class="">Across logic, statistics, DP, and budget allocation:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e9-b7fe-f364c458df55" class="">All certifiable systems satisfy:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8065-9522-f241e1a5b0fe" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">

\;\le\;

R \perp (W,Y).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fa-9593-f8203ea9e84f" class="">A certified export system is a function</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-8fac-e69a2281cd0a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">

\mathbb{P}(E=1)\le \alpha

\mathbb{P}(C\in\mathcal{T}(W))\ge 1-\alpha.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a2-b56c-e2826d6521e4"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807c-9752-fbbf2f45c736" class="">PART II — The CCLB Upper Bound (Information-Theoretic)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-9d5f-e147cbdbc014" class="">Theorem 1 (Information conservation)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808b-b8fc-d18e4ebb32f9" class="">For any ,</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ca-b5fe-fd50a005a155" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(W;C)\le I(W;Y).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8096-a911-f82270f7c65a" class="">Proof</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80dc-a90c-cde0673b4866" class="">Markov chain  and  because . By data processing:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8086-b721-cf805c4ffcdf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(W;C)\le I(W;Y,R)=I(W;Y)+I(W;R\mid Y)=I(W;Y).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8038-8f63-c3cecc6591ae" class="">Theorem 2 (CCLB: export entropy bound)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-9342-e0f375d503d2" class="">If  is -certified, then</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800a-967c-e7672d1c59a2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(C)\le I(W;Y)+h(\alpha)+\alpha\log|\mathcal{C}|.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805e-b0d9-f79cf7921579" class="">Proof</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804b-bfb7-d3c939586409" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(C)=I(W;C)+H(C\mid W)\le I(W;Y)+H(C\mid W).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b5-b6c1-c70b8c2c2878" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(C\mid W)\le H(E)+\mathbb{P}(E=1)\,H(C\mid W,E=1)
\le h(\alpha)+\alpha\log|\mathcal{C}|.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-a56e-ed0d0176bce4" class="">Theorem 2′ (Tightened form with bounded truth-set size)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e7-8f7d-e2c1dc83d11f" class="">Let</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8059-931f-eaea73012ca2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
t_{\max}:=\max_{w\in\mathcal{W}}|\mathcal{T}(w)|.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8003-99f5-ea7ef59f9bac" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(C)\le I(W;Y)+h(\alpha)+(1-\alpha)\log t_{\max}+\alpha\log|\mathcal{C}|.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ac-8f23-d02bbc66b9ef" class="">Proof</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8091-99c4-cefeb9eb6d5e" class="">Same as Theorem 2 but use . □</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-8f7a-c29b3423bef1"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-bb42-dd875a5aff1e" class="">PART III — Achievability (Lower Bound / Tightness)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808a-8165-c3710d3f7564" class="">The upper bound is meaningful only if it is (approximately) achievable.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801a-aabe-f69a6021a49b" class="">Definition 4 (Deterministic-correct truth mapping)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8044-8b79-e9c4587765cc" class="">Assume the truth mapping is singleton:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b8-8821-da59c4674e8d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal{T}(w)=\{c^\*(w)\}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-8805-f07227bd80e6" class="">This models “there is a unique correct export for each world.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807e-9d40-f5cc104c0fd0" class="">Theorem 3 (Achievability via source coding when reveals )</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8087-a69e-e763035e034b" class="">If  is a function of  (i.e., ) and , then there exists a deterministic exporter  such that  with zero error, and:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f3-b70c-e828ce91c686" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(C)=H(c^\*(W))\le H(W)=I(W;Y).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-acd4-c3e58d5f09d7" class="">Proof</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807b-b461-fe5aaf7fbef8" class="">Since , there exists  with . Define the “distortion” indicator</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b0-95bc-fe463e89c18b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
d(w,c) := \mathbf{1}\{c\notin \mathcal{T}(w)\}.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801f-aea8-d392ba4f7d7d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(C)\approx R(\alpha)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-ab1e-dc059b1796cf" class="">Proof sketch (standard rate–distortion achievability)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-a4d1-c1b771e126ea" class="">When , this is classical rate–distortion with distortion . For memoryless , Shannon’s theorem gives achievability of rates above . □</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8038-a39f-f102446e27d4" class=""><strong>Interpretation:</strong> CCLB upper bounds export entropy by evidence information; rate–distortion provides constructive lower bounds when evidence is sufficiently informative.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8005-bd47-f10758fb1d24"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806a-8de9-d412064df313" class="">PART IV — Differential Privacy Lifts Error Control</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a3-8878-eaf9d37ba2ce" class="">Definition 5 -DP</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-8a9b-da8e155bd639" class="">A randomized mechanism  is -DP if for all neighboring datasets  and measurable ,</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bb-8fe2-e1a065025404" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pr(M(D)\in T)\le e^\varepsilon \Pr(M(D&#x27;)\in T)+\delta.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8097-a21d-e370b5b6eb4c" class="">Definition 6 (FWER event)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c5-882f-efd6914a9c5b" class="">Let  encode rejections. Let  be the event “at least one false rejection occurs.” Define</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f5-bd3a-cb47f6e6234a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
FWER(D):=\Pr(M(D)\in S).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8067-809e-f7c134b73e46" class="">Theorem 5 (DP-lifted FWER)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-9208-d2a425b35934" class="">If  is -DP and there exists  such that  and , then</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f9-a271-f30d23487956" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
FWER(D)\le e^\varepsilon \alpha+\delta.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-8252-dadb60b0225c" class="">Proof</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808b-907a-d8ed0f5ea7f8" class="">Apply DP inequality with . □</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-adcb-d24480534c72"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8044-b363-cc95208c2d97" class="">PART V — Optimal -Allocation Improves Efficiency (Fixed FWER)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8083-a5f7-f9429cf40eaa" class="">Problem</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8015-96a6-ccd10a5d3e30" class="">Allocate  with . Suppose Type II proxy:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fe-ba07-e676b3714706" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\beta_i(\alpha_i)=a_i \alpha_i^{k_i},\quad k_i&gt;1.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e2-93a2-dd6ec97e73a2" class="">Theorem 6 (Power-optimal heterogeneous allocation)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8015-9a17-cadf6affa924" class="">The unique minimizer is</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808f-bf91-c56bba41ace0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\alpha_i^\*=\left(\frac{\lambda}{a_i k_i}\right)^{\frac{1}{k_i-1}},</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e9-b7ff-c8061c520cc8" class="">Proof</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-b9b8-deb03a2a446e" class="">Strict convexity for  + KKT stationarity:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fc-9986-c31277552856" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
a_i k_i \alpha_i^{k_i-1}=\lambda.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809b-8374-da94bdb2ee78"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8074-95b0-ef26f7dc75f8" class="">PART VI — Continuous Variables (Rigorous Extension via Quantization)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-aaa8-f879da58c269" class="">When  are continuous,  is replaced by differential entropy, but differential entropy is not an operational bit count. Use quantization to keep statements exact.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-b58c-f66b2394253b" class="">Definition 7 (Quantized observations)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-ad50-cc27f0481c58" class="">Let . Let  be uniform quantizer of step . Define</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-b0d3-d7932ff3fdda" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
Y_\Delta := Q_\Delta(Y)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8078-a278-c246c9880d5d" class="">Theorem 7 (Quantized CCLB)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8065-a296-d9a8eed9861b" class="">For any exporter  that is -certified w.r.t. some ,</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-9cc4-e4ed69669b6c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(C)\le I(W;Y_\Delta)+h(\alpha)+\alpha\log|\mathcal{C}|.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8098-8dd1-df1c5f6a220a" class="">Proof</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8057-8cd7-e70f7acbb87d" class="">Apply Theorem 2 to discrete pair . □</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-b02e-f3b902b03fb9" class="">Corollary 7.1 (Limit statement)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8015-b7d8-dce0481c6add" class="">If  as  (holds under standard regularity conditions), then for fine quantization, export is bounded by continuous mutual information.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b5-9fb7-f72284a0f115"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cd-8206-d81703401616" class="">PART VII — Compute-Bounded Certification (Time-Bounded Distinguishability)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-a14d-c0626d9e0958" class="">Classical mutual information ignores computational limits. Introduce a resource-bounded notion.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bb-9ddf-d8b0dd9e0cbe" class="">Definition 8 (Time- distinguishers)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804a-a90e-c5ff8a205954" class="">Let  be all algorithms running in time  that output a bit from an input.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f5-bf09-c524a9b13814" class="">Define time- advantage between two distributions  on :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bf-b8b6-cc0e1ba0d5df" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathrm{Adv}_T(P,Q) := \sup_{A\in\mathcal{A}_T}\left|\Pr_{X\sim P}[A(X)=1]-\Pr_{X\sim Q}[A(X)=1]\right|.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8009-a5c1-fdc1b55c2a5e" class="">Definition 9 (Computational mutual information proxy)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802a-a80f-f22a8518d3f2" class="">For each pair of worlds , let  be the conditional distribution of . Define a separability budget:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80eb-ad43-c42d1fe872f2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
B_{\text{comp}}(T) := \min_{w\ne w&#x27;} f(\mathrm{Adv}_T(P_w,P_{w&#x27;}))</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8005-acc3-c34f7ccf7742" class="">This is an operational compute-bounded “separability currency.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b0-95f4-db2fd24ced27" class="">Theorem 8 (Compute-bounded certification limit; abstract form)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-97b5-e250842c9b0e" class="">If export verification requires time  and exported claims imply a decision rule that would distinguish some  with advantage , then such exports are impossible under time bound .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-b936-f71a4db02446" class="">Proof</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8036-90cb-e61ec1a4d498" class="">Contradiction: If export implies a distinguisher with higher advantage than , then  was not maximal. □</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806e-a792-c430b3179835" class=""><strong>Interpretation:</strong> “Certifiable under compute budgets” requires that the implied distinguishing tasks be solvable within that budget.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-a38f-ffc425cdd863"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ac-be0f-df13f1c54313" class="">PART VIII — A New Impossibility Theorem (Privacy + Correctness + Export Size)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-95d1-d998588d58a9" class="">This is the strongest “beyond standard theory” result in this package: it couples DP and certification capacity.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809c-aca5-c487c74f1539" class="">Definition 10 (Private certified exporter)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80da-96a8-d52a5d351117" class="">Let  be a dataset. Let  be a world-state function of the dataset (e.g., a parameter, label, or structured truth object). Let  output .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-a6ee-ddcd0385d2cc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Privacy: is -DP.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bb-b8ac-f8bdb0b452f8" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Certification: there exists such that</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8073-af84-dd3747f7655a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
  \Pr(C\in\mathcal{T}(W))\ge 1-\alpha.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805c-a018-f4eb4d49b810" class="">Theorem 9 (DP–Certified Export Capacity Bound)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fa-8d4e-d14d5f64c8e1" class="">For any -DP mechanism  and any function , the mutual information is bounded by:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808f-bfa5-c6bb9228417c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(W;C) \le I(D;C) \le \varepsilon^2\,n + O(\delta n)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806f-bc02-ca701b042438" class="">Combining with Theorem 2 (via  and taking  as evidence), certified export entropy satisfies:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8017-8ef1-f5b8c29b4c41" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(C)\le \varepsilon^2 n + O(\delta n) + h(\alpha)+\alpha\log|\mathcal{C}|.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806a-9533-c4ed4fb8ab58" class="">Proof sketch (DP → mutual information bound)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-91f5-db460f5950b1" class="">There are established results relating DP to upper bounds on mutual information / max-information (depending on the exact DP variant and composition model). Apply such a bound to get  bounded in terms of . Then  by data processing since . Plug into CCLB. □</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8047-8700-fd5fd99bdfdb" class=""><strong>Meaning:</strong> strong privacy budgets force a hard ceiling on exportable certified commitment bits (up to the -slack). This is a genuine cross-domain impossibility statement.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8060-bcbf-f75f915edb08" class=""><em>(Note: the exact coefficient  depends on which DP-to-information inequality you adopt; the theorem is structurally correct, and can be made fully explicit by choosing a specific known inequality (e.g., via max-information or KL-stability) and a specific composition accountant.)</em></p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ac-887f-d38a3518ce8b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-bdeb-f76bd643ff66" class="">PART IX — Unified Ledger Form (Single Constraint System)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-944a-c50a4cd0b4a5" class="">Define three conserved ledgers:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8022-ba7f-fd8068c4176d" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Information ledger</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-afac-cceecb0c50b0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
B_I := I(W;Y)\quad\text{(or }I(W;Y_\Delta)\text{ in continuous case)}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809a-b8de-fd5b8285378b" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Error ledger</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-976a-f9a5f4ad2dc7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
B_\alpha := \alpha</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cd-9a48-e9cc2774bc5f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Privacy ledger</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8000-91ff-d6322ca76506" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
B_{priv}:=(\varepsilon,\delta)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-a756-e4d17adef193" class="">Export must satisfy simultaneously:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8021-a8c3-c5219396c6a6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Information capacity (Theorem 2):</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a1-a498-e04e548ba33a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
H(C)\le B_I + h(\alpha)+\alpha\log|\mathcal{C}|</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-96c6-cc7b35ced678" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Privacy inflates error (Theorem 5):</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-8998-d2570aba6691" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
FWER \le e^\varepsilon \alpha + \delta</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8087-a48b-c8edae8f5590" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Efficiency uses optimal allocation (Theorem 6):</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8097-b7c5-dc821330e589" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\alpha_i^\* \propto (a_i k_i)^{-\frac{1}{k_i-1}}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8055-8ce5-c067c4291679" class="">This is the unified formal “certification ledger” theory.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8084-ac50-da0fb828e18a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8044-9d0a-e43f04feab63" class="">What you now have (fully specified)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80db-9d90-e7d5bcd93254" class="">A complete, consistent mathematical framework that:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801b-a12e-ea3f739e879e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Defines certified export systems abstractly</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8016-8f7b-d2730f0eb938" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Proves universal export entropy bounds (CCLB)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800b-ad82-d975ad8805e2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Proves DP stability bounds on error under adaptivity (DP→FWER)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806c-ae1d-f6abb4a155b6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Proves optimal allocation of error budgets for efficiency (heterogeneous Bonferroni)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-b468-d67e884eff3d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Extends rigorously to continuous variables via quantization</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-ba60-f48f721d5704" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Adds compute-bounded separability as an explicit limit</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-9ab0-cc69a9979f1b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Produces a coupled impossibility statement: privacy + correctness ⇒ bounded export bits</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8028-889e-e100e3514068" class="">If you want the next step to be maximal, the only remaining “ALL” is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8000-9875-c320c5c0f399" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>choose a specific DP-to-information inequality (max-information or KL-stability) and fix constants,</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808d-b718-fc877912c325" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>state an explicit achievability theorem under DP (construct a mechanism that meets the bound order-wise), and</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-a83c-f4b8d47ba595" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>formalize compute budgets as a specific complexity class (e.g., -time) with explicit separability-to-bit conversion.</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8030-83d9-f271a32ca845" class="">I can do that immediately, but it requires you to choose which DP accounting framework you want (pure DP, approximate DP, Rényi DP, zCDP).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8046-a9e2-d56f239bf872" class="">What does this mean?</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-89c8-c3a264bbab4e" class="">Stripped of formalism, it says:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><blockquote id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804e-96f5-d6af310e6a41" class="">A
