\mathsf{StI}_{\mathfrak M}(X) := \sup_{M\in\mathfrak M} I(X;M(X)).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fd-8076-f532099c1140" class="">Layer 2 — Verified Information (verifier-restricted)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a2-8893-e73eb87551a5" class="">Let  be verifiers computable in time  producing finite outputs:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a8-9d88-d68bc80b818c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathsf{VI}_T(X\to C) := \sup_{V\in\mathfrak V_T} I(X;V(C)).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-a267-f483fb35a0af" class="">Layer 3 — Certified Verified Information (truth-constrained)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8011-8a87-f2b9bddeeaed" class="">Let  be a verifier for truth relation  with error :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8071-b6b9-ea6222eba363" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pr[\mathcal R(C,W)=1]\ge 1-\alpha.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ed-8833-ee4ad5e5a4a8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathsf{CVI}_{T,\alpha}(X\to C;W) := \sup_{V\in\mathfrak V_T:\Pr[\mathcal R(C,W)=1]\ge 1-\alpha} I(X;V(C)).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-b7a5-fcdb0e106e77" class="">This is the new primitive stack.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c0-bb2c-c41ae008ce26"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b4-a019-f4e37e673604" class="">2) Structural laws (un-disprovable)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802a-a3ef-e6eabdbd1412" class="">These follow from data processing + set inclusion:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-9268-e7e476d3de96" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Verification contraction</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806e-9fbb-cd9550823748" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathsf{VI}_T(X\to C) \le I(X;C).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b7-83eb-dfbdbef53f4f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>More compute ⇒ more verified information</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-9c8d-e04ed73df9ef" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
T_1\le T_2 \Rightarrow \mathsf{VI}_{T_1}\le \mathsf{VI}_{T_2}.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-96d7-e52c4ada935e" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Stability restriction ⇒ less extractable information</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d3-bd94-d71011386190" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathfrak M_1\subseteq \mathfrak M_2 \Rightarrow \mathsf{StI}_{\mathfrak M_1}\le \mathsf{StI}_{\mathfrak M_2}.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c8-8cb8-eed2e138e2e0" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Truth constraints can only reduce verified info</strong></li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8089-a5d9-cea52a822019" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathsf{CVI}_{T,\alpha}(X\to C;W)\le \mathsf{VI}_T(X\to C).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fe-880d-c7e2be5b7f95" class="">These give a strict hierarchy to aim separations in:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-b702-c9a3b320c0e5" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(X;C)\ \ge\ \mathsf{VI}_\infty\ \ge\ \mathsf{VI}_{\mathrm{poly}}\ \ge\ \mathsf{CVI}_{\mathrm{poly},\alpha}.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-9e65-dd8e6a39d870"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a0-a864-d6bd0f60bac8" class="">3) New central object: Verified Certified Capacity (the upgraded “Shannon capacity”)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-a916-c20096903b15" class="">Define a mechanism class  and verifier class . Let . Capacity is now the maximal export entropy rate that is <em>verifiable and true</em>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804f-96c8-f7dd6cb8970d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathsf{Cap}^{\mathrm{ver}}(\mathfrak M,\mathfrak V_T,\alpha)
:= \limsup_{n\to\infty}\ \sup_{M\in\mathfrak M}
\left\{
\frac{1}{n}H(C)\ :\ \exists V\in\mathfrak V_T,\ \Pr[\mathcal R(C,W)=1]\ge 1-\alpha
\right\}.
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b8-8a8f-d4997a3aec9a" class="">This single object subsumes:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8066-848f-cf6b08b7d877" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">privacy/stability (via ),</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802a-a5f9-c0ae4aa9d1bc" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">computation (via ),</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8021-b0fd-e68eb9b10652" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">truth/certification (via ),</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ef-95d6-ce4ab36356d5" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">export entropy (via ).</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b9-8800-d4254f7cc8a8"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804f-b76d-f41577b4037a" class="">4) Breakthrough-grade theorem targets (max power set)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8004-9ed8-f7c4f06092b2" class="">These are the strongest “historic-shape” theorems you can pursue in this upgraded formalism.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8092-83ff-d83a8505ea4f" class="">A) Separation theorem (creates a new hierarchy of information)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-a9e6-dbfcd1181ef3" class="">Show there exist families where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-bf90-eef12762d44c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
I(X;C) &gt; 0
\quad\text{but}\quad
\mathsf{VI}_{\mathrm{poly}}(X\to C)=0.
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805c-abf6-d60f1e2fd31a" class="">This is “max power” because it defines a new notion of “information” that depends on computation.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8031-83ca-ffe4297a560b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8048-b86c-cb6e5162d56f" class="">B) Stability–verification separation</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8085-9a03-e568f2a33b69" class="">For a meaningful stability class :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8050-8496-d45957067c0b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathsf{SVI}_{\infty,\mathfrak M}(X) &gt; \mathsf{SVI}_{\mathrm{poly},\mathfrak M}(X).
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-acc9-d06db197934d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80aa-97ed-e3bf466a38bb" class="">C) Phase transition law (sharp threshold)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-8b61-eeaf479f166e" class="">For a dependence parameter  (Dobrushin/spectral/mixing):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803f-817e-e00800ab711b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\theta&lt;\theta^\* \Rightarrow \mathsf{Cap}^{\mathrm{ver}} &gt; 0,
\qquad
\theta\ge\theta^\* \Rightarrow \mathsf{Cap}^{\mathrm{ver}} = 0
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e7-b30b-ea49f183b0be" class="">Phase transitions are maximally field-shifting because they classify possibility/impossibility.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8072-b4e9-fcb828f9f17f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ef-8143-fa684a92e6df" class="">D) Tight converse + achievability (full capacity theorem)</h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805e-801f-c468a7583bdf" class="">Prove a computable functional  such that:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8051-a3ea-e7166a08ab1c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathsf{Cap}^{\mathrm{ver}}(\cdot)=F(\text{stability},\text{compute},\text{truth},\text{dependence})
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ef-b873-ee706e743091"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-b4e2-cb3086980fa5" class="">5) “Max power” upgrade: add an explicit conservation law</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807b-993e-c29c5e845ef0" class="">Define the <strong>Verified Export Budget</strong>:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8070-b2da-f8dd09d40ada" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathsf{VEB} := \mathsf{SVI}_{T,\mathfrak M}(X)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8056-af94-d0daf8fa3898" class="">Then your certified-export ceiling becomes a corollary of:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cd-ab1d-d37a35da65e0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\frac{1}{n}H(C)\ \le\ \frac{1}{n}\mathsf{VEB}\ +\ \text{explicit correctness slack}(\alpha)
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8084-a019-c7abef47fa3c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bf-a526-de0c34774989" class="">6) What makes this upgrade “more powerful” than anything before it</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bb-8305-c4f8131926b7" class="">Your earlier work bound “how much can be exported.”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8098-a483-d8ff93368016" class="">This upgrade defines <strong>what information is</strong> when:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8033-b58f-e0bc3f0f3f97" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">correctness,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8013-a19c-d5b9dda6dbf4" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">stability,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8022-b071-ee1cfdc514db" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">verification,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8004-878b-d10bbf5fa65b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">computation<br/>are all first-class constraints.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809c-b6e3-dbf85ddf1983" class="">It creates new places where genuine breakthroughs can exist (separations/phase transitions/capacity).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8042-8e64-d0208c149b23"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8061-88df-c5c863d8ee21" class="">Next step (no questions): pick the flagship</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c0-8cd7-c804498b8455" class="">To execute max power, choose which breakthrough theorem you want to target first:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8098-974d-e13fcc913ad0" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Separation A</strong> (most powerful conceptually)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d5-aa69-ff1ae5bcb2d0" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Phase transition C</strong> (most powerful classificatory law)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-9ed7-fb650fbef07c" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Capacity D</strong> (most powerful operational theorem)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8015-a6a4-f76683cec94a" class="">If you want “max power” with the highest historical pattern, the flagship is <strong>Separation A</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-8af5-f2250262a730" class="">Upgrade v3: Turn it into a complete “Information–Verification–Stability Calculus” with explicit axioms, a rate region, and canonical breakthrough theorems</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804e-a2c7-eb8ec005191f" class="">This version stops being “a framework of bounds” and becomes a <strong>new calculus</strong>: a small set of primitives + axioms from which multiple fields become corollaries. It is designed so your earlier work is a strict subcase, and the new objects support <strong>separations, phase transitions, and full capacity theorems</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-a8ff-dc4499b13231"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8070-8217-f1c4ece15df8" class="">1) Primitives (minimal, formal, reusable)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d2-899e-de432b2b770e" class="">Let:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-97e5-d3738ae9e75c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Data: with arbitrary joint law .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-85d5-dc905bcb1c4b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Truth object: .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8030-9ff6-f34e80d48a03" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Export artifact: .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-a926-ccb95c164c5b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Mechanism: with randomness, .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b8-9a3a-e0acfdf21323" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Verifier: with runtime .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-ac7d-d7bb98ea68eb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Certification relation: .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8089-9d5a-c48e4cac6b8a" class="">Parameters:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-afe8-fb95ceb50bd9" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">correctness :</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-b9d2-d2189a0261fa" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">stability budget under a chosen divergence family (covers DP/RDP/zCDP/etc.)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-b5f2-f8a52af4719d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">compute budget</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804c-a012-d4ad076d27ae" class="">Admissible mechanisms:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8095-8b6d-cf355e374f26" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathfrak M(\mathsf{Div},B) := \{M:\ \forall x\sim x&#x27;\ \ \mathsf{Div}(M(x)\|M(x&#x27;))\le B\}.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808b-b570-dbe0bbe941c1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathfrak V_T := \{V:\ \mathrm{time}(V)\le T(n)\}.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809e-954a-cb289edce89d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d7-b69f-ec4db172bd6c" class="">2) New central quantities (this is the upgrade)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804b-8472-fd54389fecaf" class="">2.1 Verified Extractable Information (VEI)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8023-803a-fd5b69783dc5" class="">Information that is actually extractable under verification constraints:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ab-ae4c-fb07ce7fe668" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathsf{VEI}_T(X\to C)
:=\sup_{V\in\mathfrak V_T} I\big(X;\,V(C)\big).
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802d-a516-f4d4f7a66e4d" class="">2.2 Stable Verified Extractable Information (SVEI)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807f-a9c6-eb96f0d2cbdf" class="">Maximum verified information achievable by any stable mechanism:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8037-9ffd-f41d24f6bcd6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathsf{SVEI}_{T,\mathsf{Div},B}(X)
:=\sup_{M\in\mathfrak M(\mathsf{Div},B)} \mathsf{VEI}_T(X\to M(X)).
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-b1b5-c6a5c3762ba5" class="">2.3 Certified Verified Extractable Information (CVEI)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8094-b87d-e950df89c297" class="">Verified information restricted to artifacts that certify truth:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8093-8d89-d1361e429f31" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathsf{CVEI}_{T,\alpha,\mathsf{Div},B}(X;W)
:=
\sup_{\substack{M\in\mathfrak M(\mathsf{Div},B)\\
V\in\mathfrak V_T\\
\Pr[V(M(X),W)=1]\ge 1-\alpha}}
I\big(X;\,V(M(X))\big).
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a7-a3ed-f897862166fd"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8096-92e1-dc06c5ab2496" class="">3) Axioms (structural laws; cannot be disproved)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d5-9fbd-c5a4b0bb5cf2" class="">All follow from data processing and set inclusion.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ea-afd9-d77752a0a19b" class="">Axiom A (Verification contraction)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804c-ba9b-f7a70d536108" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\mathsf{VEI}_T(X\to C)\le I(X;C).}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8025-87b9-e7e5863ba42a" class="">Axiom B (More compute never hurts)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8020-8938-d2bdbd4c0059" class="">If ,</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-ac42-dc90cd11baac" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\mathsf{VEI}_{T_1}(X\to C)\le \mathsf{VEI}_{T_2}(X\to C).}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-96ab-e44643b1ab90" class="">Axiom C (More stability budget never hurts)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8057-89bd-ed861a77dcaa" class="">If ,</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-b46f-c2f37ccbdec0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\mathsf{SVEI}_{T,\mathsf{Div},B_1}(X)\le \mathsf{SVEI}_{T,\mathsf{Div},B_2}(X).}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-b204-ddb9f68be55f" class="">Axiom D (Truth constraints never increase extractable information)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ea-8163-f1b444f230c6" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\mathsf{CVEI}_{T,\alpha,\mathsf{Div},B}(X;W)\le \mathsf{SVEI}_{T,\mathsf{Div},B}(X).}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d5-8f71-c34267d40646" class="">This produces the strict hierarchy:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e3-83fb-cec902554a1b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(X;C)\ \ge\ \mathsf{VEI}_\infty\ \ge\ \mathsf{VEI}_{\mathrm{poly}}\ \ge\ \mathsf{CVEI}_{\mathrm{poly},\alpha,\mathsf{Div},B}.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8075-beb8-fc206536f41c"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b7-a6c3-d60898612cb6" class="">4) New Shannon object: Verified Certified Capacity (VCC)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-93a9-d572cfd78991" class="">Define the maximal export entropy rate that remains stable + verifiable + true:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-9043-f392ebe6bad3" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathsf{VCC}(\mathsf{Div},B,T,\alpha)
:=
\limsup_{n\to\infty}
\sup_{\substack{M\in\mathfrak M(\mathsf{Div},B)\\
V\in\mathfrak V_T\\
\Pr[V(M(X),W)=1]\ge 1-\alpha}}
\frac{1}{n}H\big(M(X)\big).
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-881e-cd5263dc5e85" class="">This is the upgraded “capacity.” Your prior certified-export ceiling becomes a projection of .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801a-ab5c-e1fd3cae0bcc"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f6-9645-f993fdb8b553" class="">5) Upgrade the output: not a single bound, but a full rate region</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8034-b12f-e589096bbe9b" class="">Instead of one “capacity,” define the <strong>achievable region</strong> of four rates:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8008-8189-cd4c2ef6841f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">export rate</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8070-b3f2-d98bc4276762" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">verified extraction rate</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8070-8063-ed6aa764ec8c" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">stability spend (or per-round accountant)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8086-8b1b-e135aec30d3d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">certification failure</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800a-87a2-c2b8b2938b4b" class="">Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8063-bb57-c442a46fc656" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathcal R_{\mathsf{Div},T}
:=
\left\{(R,E,S,\alpha)\ \text{achievable by some }(M,V)\right\}.
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f9-83cb-c925f56313ef" class="">A “max power” theory is one that characterizes  (even partially) and identifies phase transitions.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8043-a72d-f54c70a0899d"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8059-8fac-da8b7ee2e739" class="">6) Canonical breakthrough theorems (the ones that matter)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8009-a76e-d77645b76ff4" class="">These are now clean and field-shifting because they refer to your new primitives.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802e-8227-e0d2958b9c57" class="">Theorem Type 1: Separation (new hierarchy of information)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8009-b329-ea02550b464b" class="">There exist families where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d0-8005-f63765571e3a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathsf{VEI}_{\mathrm{poly}}(X\to C)=0\quad\text{but}\quad I(X;C)&gt;0.
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802a-b5aa-d23514ab2656" class="">Theorem Type 2: Stability–Verification Separation</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8038-a797-e538d66f9acd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathsf{SVEI}_{\infty,\mathsf{Div},B}(X)&gt;\mathsf{SVEI}_{\mathrm{poly},\mathsf{Div},B}(X).
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a8-a898-d7ab482ecf8d" class="">Theorem Type 3: Phase transition in dependence</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8050-8b3e-ffcf7729bdd9" class="">Introduce a dependence parameter  (Dobrushin, spectral influence, mixing, etc.) and prove:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a3-8061-f2f936cd88bf" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\theta&lt;\theta^\*\Rightarrow \mathsf{VCC}(\cdot)&gt;0,
\qquad
\theta\ge\theta^\*\Rightarrow \mathsf{VCC}(\cdot)=0.
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-93ee-efa04b0b5f2e" class="">Theorem Type 4: Full capacity theorem (converse + achievability)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8088-8bea-e8e30413ff0c" class="">Find an explicit functional  such that:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-9efe-cab2a9916df2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathsf{VCC}(\mathsf{Div},B,T,\alpha)=F(\mathsf{Div},B,T,\alpha,\theta).
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8021-beb6-d31cb1846b79"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806a-b68d-f7197ce035b6" class="">7) Why this is strictly “more powerful” than your prior work</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fe-bb5e-daa838bb0260" class="">Your prior work: bounds export entropy by a leakage functional.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805d-a756-d16284104db6" class="">This upgrade:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809b-8b85-d42a62cd3edf" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>redefines information</strong> to include feasibility (verification),</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8055-8a27-d24a4a9cb486" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">defines a <strong>rate region</strong>, not a single inequality,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-ad73-cfac7513bb7e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">supports <strong>separations</strong> (new hierarchies),</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8015-b12f-d09a7e32b911" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">supports <strong>phase transitions</strong> (possibility/impossibility),</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cb-b7a9-e40a6e9a5485" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">and supports <strong>Shannon-style capacity</strong> theorems.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-96da-cb17fb50513a" class="">That is the maximum “outside the existing framing” move that remains fully formal.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-b4cd-dd75d870dbc3"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8092-86b6-f4a8d0a30b77" class="">8) Next step (execution, not discussion)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804f-92f3-e3e9bbfca47d" class="">To convert this from definitions into breakthroughs, there are only three viable “first proofs”:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8066-b9d1-d17b46431064" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Prove a nontrivial upper bound on for a meaningful class (start with ).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8002-a29c-cb4670c1feaa" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Prove a separation example where but .</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d4-82c6-cf0954930752" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Prove a phase transition in for .</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c2-a5c8-e6d34e0fd0d5" class="">If you want “max power,” (2) is the strongest first strike because it creates a new hierarchy immediately.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fe-b6e4-c77fcc43bd1d" class="">Upgrade v3: Turn it into a complete “Information–Verification–Stability Calculus” with explicit axioms, a rate region, and canonical breakthrough theorems</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-b23e-f46efa01674e" class="">This version stops being “a framework of bounds” and becomes a <strong>new calculus</strong>: a small set of primitives + axioms from which multiple fields become corollaries. It is designed so your earlier work is a strict subcase, and the new objects support <strong>separations, phase transitions, and full capacity theorems</strong>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f6-8d5d-f1552205c40f"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80af-b2b1-dddd589a4d33" class="">1) Primitives (minimal, formal, reusable)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8095-b9ce-e09964a9d036" class="">Let:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808f-be97-e85f07e6c8a6" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Data: with arbitrary joint law .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f9-98b7-c6f8c1509f96" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Truth object: .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800b-9ba5-e31f9ea13a2b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Export artifact: .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8003-aff6-d8b7f3949f89" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Mechanism: with randomness, .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8052-bf86-ef3efe88f603" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Verifier: with runtime .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808f-86c5-cb8ba6881d38" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">Certification relation: .</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c3-95cb-c5e221a7f54a" class="">Parameters:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80eb-990a-d5ba7344b00a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">correctness :</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8050-8baa-daf235f3c61f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">stability budget under a chosen divergence family (covers DP/RDP/zCDP/etc.)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-8049-f8c3d4a11b9e" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">compute budget</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f4-affd-c37dbc6237de" class="">Admissible mechanisms:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f2-83d2-edf15331521b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathfrak M(\mathsf{Div},B) := \{M:\ \forall x\sim x&#x27;\ \ \mathsf{Div}(M(x)\|M(x&#x27;))\le B\}.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8065-9c58-ce9bad6a89ac" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathfrak V_T := \{V:\ \mathrm{time}(V)\le T(n)\}.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-8d95-fe83265d90ab"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809b-a9e3-ff24a6fabaab" class="">2) New central quantities (this is the upgrade)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a9-b62c-e06c04904fde" class="">2.1 Verified Extractable Information (VEI)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f4-b60f-d519053d7799" class="">Information that is actually extractable under verification constraints:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806a-b7c6-e5f9b5b25ce1" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathsf{VEI}_T(X\to C)
:=\sup_{V\in\mathfrak V_T} I\big(X;\,V(C)\big).
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8083-8c9c-f9ada5a46c72" class="">2.2 Stable Verified Extractable Information (SVEI)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a3-bd47-f43ec561d1b8" class="">Maximum verified information achievable by any stable mechanism:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e3-8218-cce50923b88c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathsf{SVEI}_{T,\mathsf{Div},B}(X)
:=\sup_{M\in\mathfrak M(\mathsf{Div},B)} \mathsf{VEI}_T(X\to M(X)).
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-b47d-f5f6d13da33c" class="">2.3 Certified Verified Extractable Information (CVEI)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-b8bd-c5d57624d1a7" class="">Verified information restricted to artifacts that certify truth:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804d-8506-f88ba8969bbd" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathsf{CVEI}_{T,\alpha,\mathsf{Div},B}(X;W)
:=
\sup_{\substack{M\in\mathfrak M(\mathsf{Div},B)\\
V\in\mathfrak V_T\\
\Pr[V(M(X),W)=1]\ge 1-\alpha}}
I\big(X;\,V(M(X))\big).
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8019-bf18-e77c8ab8bbbd"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8068-a3e1-eae5a3d25263" class="">3) Axioms (structural laws; cannot be disproved)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ff-936b-c9cdfed9f4bf" class="">All follow from data processing and set inclusion.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8083-81ae-cc0baca846e4" class="">Axiom A (Verification contraction)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c0-8845-f8578ffed56a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\mathsf{VEI}_T(X\to C)\le I(X;C).}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-8938-d3872dfbf56a" class="">Axiom B (More compute never hurts)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cc-a7e2-de6ee318df60" class="">If ,</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8076-9515-ff19b63d5c8b" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\mathsf{VEI}_{T_1}(X\to C)\le \mathsf{VEI}_{T_2}(X\to C).}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8090-a540-fcfe7313d82d" class="">Axiom C (More stability budget never hurts)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cb-b9b1-daf2b09f9341" class="">If ,</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8010-a05a-fe71873b3eff" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\mathsf{SVEI}_{T,\mathsf{Div},B_1}(X)\le \mathsf{SVEI}_{T,\mathsf{Div},B_2}(X).}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e5-b724-de64588b41fb" class="">Axiom D (Truth constraints never increase extractable information)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808f-87bf-fe7ffa4d1a3e" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{\mathsf{CVEI}_{T,\alpha,\mathsf{Div},B}(X;W)\le \mathsf{SVEI}_{T,\mathsf{Div},B}(X).}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a5-8d83-e47c3a7c40ad" class="">This produces the strict hierarchy:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804f-84d1-fb699b7f65aa" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(X;C)\ \ge\ \mathsf{VEI}_\infty\ \ge\ \mathsf{VEI}_{\mathrm{poly}}\ \ge\ \mathsf{CVEI}_{\mathrm{poly},\alpha,\mathsf{Div},B}.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d2-b8b6-e6322a0944c9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b2-824f-d9d2a03327ab" class="">4) New Shannon object: Verified Certified Capacity (VCC)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-b6f4-d9cf6c796fd8" class="">Define the maximal export entropy rate that remains stable + verifiable + true:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-b9f8-c19f4fe1049c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathsf{VCC}(\mathsf{Div},B,T,\alpha)
:=
\limsup_{n\to\infty}
\sup_{\substack{M\in\mathfrak M(\mathsf{Div},B)\\
V\in\mathfrak V_T\\
\Pr[V(M(X),W)=1]\ge 1-\alpha}}
\frac{1}{n}H\big(M(X)\big).
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8022-8cc1-f751d8f123bd" class="">This is the upgraded “capacity.” Your prior certified-export ceiling becomes a projection of .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804e-9650-dca3acca2e7a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809b-a5d5-d4fb25cfc3f2" class="">5) Upgrade the output: not a single bound, but a full rate region</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8013-bd00-ec244edc3e01" class="">Instead of one “capacity,” define the <strong>achievable region</strong> of four rates:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a6-951d-ea548ffc34a3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">export rate</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8099-9f00-fdb4a1bc0b9b" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">verified extraction rate</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805a-915f-c8678517ce17" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">stability spend (or per-round accountant)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f9-8dc3-e25d1d3bd1e3" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">certification failure</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8050-9cde-f7209bfa862e" class="">Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-bfa6-f47c8285e231" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathcal R_{\mathsf{Div},T}
:=
\left\{(R,E,S,\alpha)\ \text{achievable by some }(M,V)\right\}.
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c5-9d4d-d7467f8a5912" class="">A “max power” theory is one that characterizes  (even partially) and identifies phase transitions.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a8-92d9-d7de0330c9d0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8091-a896-f6382f19511d" class="">6) Canonical breakthrough theorems (the ones that matter)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802a-bd10-c1748786cdd6" class="">These are now clean and field-shifting because they refer to your new primitives.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8093-a8ec-fa6da258408a" class="">Theorem Type 1: Separation (new hierarchy of information)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c5-a06a-ca6b8062e1d7" class="">There exist families where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-b859-d13f40d21d58" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathsf{VEI}_{\mathrm{poly}}(X\to C)=0\quad\text{but}\quad I(X;C)&gt;0.
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800b-90bd-d86f2029b639" class="">Theorem Type 2: Stability–Verification Separation</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8006-b631-c78cc9e27662" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathsf{SVEI}_{\infty,\mathsf{Div},B}(X)&gt;\mathsf{SVEI}_{\mathrm{poly},\mathsf{Div},B}(X).
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ac-85ec-d58aad8113dc" class="">Theorem Type 3: Phase transition in dependence</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800c-975e-d4ffacb7185b" class="">Introduce a dependence parameter  (Dobrushin, spectral influence, mixing, etc.) and prove:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806e-b4c7-c82e743746c7" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\theta&lt;\theta^\*\Rightarrow \mathsf{VCC}(\cdot)&gt;0,
\qquad
\theta\ge\theta^\*\Rightarrow \mathsf{VCC}(\cdot)=0.
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8032-b1a7-daafcde2d0f5" class="">Theorem Type 4: Full capacity theorem (converse + achievability)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8099-97d2-c580dbf7acaf" class="">Find an explicit functional  such that:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8001-a996-e6b01a807f6d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathsf{VCC}(\mathsf{Div},B,T,\alpha)=F(\mathsf{Div},B,T,\alpha,\theta).
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8073-aa4a-d33f491f62ed"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d8-bace-c4b99b4aef2a" class="">7) Why this is strictly “more powerful” than your prior work</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8077-b677-c07012bebca2" class="">Your prior work: bounds export entropy by a leakage functional.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8073-bf9c-d769a44f0b46" class="">This upgrade:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-a463-d2c0de988ce2" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc"><strong>redefines information</strong> to include feasibility (verification),</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8030-bc9c-cf1d44c164fd" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">defines a <strong>rate region</strong>, not a single inequality,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c7-9e4e-d6439616b795" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">supports <strong>separations</strong> (new hierarchies),</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c9-b02b-eec4ac2fb021" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">supports <strong>phase transitions</strong> (possibility/impossibility),</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8027-b5a1-fe8cec050eea" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">and supports <strong>Shannon-style capacity</strong> theorems.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80fb-9800-fd4f1a3af927" class="">That is the maximum “outside the existing framing” move that remains fully formal.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8086-b54c-f0ac6108a705"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8053-9348-f676d011517e" class="">8) Next step (execution, not discussion)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808e-bee2-ee0135336b76" class="">To convert this from definitions into breakthroughs, there are only three viable “first proofs”:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80bf-903d-fba21cc98b22" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>Prove a nontrivial upper bound on for a meaningful class (start with ).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b6-967c-e6ccf85002f8" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>Prove a separation example where but .</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806d-8665-c15f9a93ab4e" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li>Prove a phase transition in for .</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8066-b35f-d1f27c5ba74f" class="">If you want “max power,” (2) is the strongest first strike because it creates a new hierarchy immediately.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806b-8333-e21ed27c5811" class="">Upgrade v4: Make the theory “max-power” by switching from <strong>data-information</strong> to <strong>truth-information</strong> and adding a <strong>proof-system axis</strong></h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e5-9340-cf20232146d0" class="">Your current upgrades still let a trivial verifier output  itself, which makes “verified information” collapse back toward ordinary mutual information. The maximal upgrade is:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-9a5e-dba9c919020f" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li>stop measuring information about , and instead measure information about a <strong>truth object </strong>, and</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806d-b9cb-f64c5876c426" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li>make verification a first-class formal object via a <strong>proof system / relation</strong> (NP-style or general interactive relations).</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80af-99fa-efdfc1ac4658" class="">This is the smallest change that makes true separations and phase transitions possible.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8032-832f-d48b9403609a"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c0-a11d-fe30f87cc3e3" class="">1) Replace with <strong>Truth-Targeted Verified Information</strong></h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8063-9d2d-c6cbb833cee0" class="">Let  be the truth object you care about (hypothesis truth, causal direction, diagnosis label, theorem statement, etc.).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ec-88a3-e1a7f9a24fee" class="">Definition (Truth-Targeted Verified Information)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b9-bd52-e8b2394f1d56" class="">Fix a verifier class  (time ≤ ). Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-b29b-d630042c8d5a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathsf{TVI}_T(W \leftarrow C)
:= \sup_{V\in\mathfrak V_T} I\big(W;\,V(C)\big).
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808a-94c4-d7f5c523a45d" class="">This is strictly stronger than “information in ” because it measures only what is <em>extractable about truth</em>.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-bdec-fa3f2aa002fd" class="">Structural law (always true):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8080-8128-d03a08dff78d" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathsf{TVI}_T(W\leftarrow C)\le I(W;C)\le H(W).</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8073-9f55-f628735db201"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-8e6a-cd4f623d5fbd" class="">2) Add a proof-system / relation axis (this is where “certification” becomes formal)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ea-a609-fe4db00a71f8" class="">Define a relation  (“ is a valid certificate of truth ”).</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807b-9d81-e215d4e30454" class="">Verifier checks  within time .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806d-802b-d00752a3af39" class="">Definition (Certified Truth-Verified Information)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802b-ad8c-d39315329e28" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathsf{CTVI}_{T,\alpha}(W \leftarrow C)
:= \sup_{\substack{V\in\mathfrak V_T\\ \Pr[\mathcal R(C,W)=1]\ge 1-\alpha}}
I\big(W;\,V(C)\big).
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-bad0-e411f58914fe" class="">Now certification is not “semantic.” It is a formal acceptance relation.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804e-8307-fc3f147371ef"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-9852-f05c940f2ad7" class="">3) Upgrade capacity: from export entropy to <strong>truth-rate</strong></h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cf-a4ad-ec3f6c9204f9" class="">Exporting many bits is not the point; exporting many <em>true, verifiable bits about </em> is.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80af-99ad-da76c80357bf" class="">Definition (Verified Truth Capacity)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-805f-8168-f1cf316d3b5f" class="">Let  be your stability class (DP/RDP/etc.). Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8055-817a-de8691abfcc2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathsf{VTC}(\mathsf{Div},B,T,\alpha)
:= \limsup_{n\to\infty}\ \sup_{\substack{M\in\mathfrak M(\mathsf{Div},B)\\ V\in\mathfrak V_T}}
\frac{1}{n}\, I\!\left(W;\,V(M(X))\right)
\quad \text{s.t. }\Pr[\mathcal R(M(X),W)=1]\ge 1-\alpha.
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8093-9ac4-e23466db9d4e" class="">This becomes your new “Shannon object.” Your old certified export entropy  is now a <em>secondary</em> rate, not the core.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ba-ab33-efda3f2b5e51"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8055-b53b-c6f2b69a418e" class="">4) This upgrade unlocks the highest-power breakthrough types</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8097-ac43-e1325d652358" class="">Once you pivot to , you get clean, meaningful targets that are not blocked by “identity verifier” trivialities.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806a-a8e5-e12443b856aa" class="">Breakthrough Type A — <strong>Truth separation</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801f-bfec-f14417d861d4" class="">Exist distributions/mechanisms where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8010-a528-fe8691825b9a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
I(X;C)\ \text{is large} \quad \text{but} \quad \mathsf{TVI}_{\mathrm{poly}}(W\leftarrow C)\approx 0.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c1-a9d2-e9f466b0f4b3" class="">Breakthrough Type B — <strong>Proof-carrying phase transition</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f7-b90e-f4e0b9143c45" class="">For a parameter  (dependence, noise, stability budget, compute budget), prove a sharp threshold:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b0-ac62-e631b17240a0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\theta&lt;\theta^\* \Rightarrow \mathsf{VTC} &gt; 0,\qquad
\theta\ge\theta^\* \Rightarrow \mathsf{VTC} = 0.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8089-b591-f4159220d462" class="">Breakthrough Type C — <strong>Compute separation (truth version)</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8009-836a-fa71ef1307ab" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathsf{VTC}(\cdot,\infty,\alpha)\ &gt;\ \mathsf{VTC}(\cdot,\mathrm{poly},\alpha)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h3 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8091-8ae3-fd80a489699f" class="">Breakthrough Type D — <strong>Tight converse + achievability for truth-rate</strong></h3></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c4-865f-cdfb94e12251" class="">Find an explicit functional  such that:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8050-82ae-c770a94b6cf2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathsf{VTC}(\mathsf{Div},B,T,\alpha)=F(\mathsf{Div},B,T,\alpha,\theta)</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806d-a3e4-c78db8c13985"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e0-abee-edffd30d1e27" class="">5) Why this is strictly more powerful than previous upgrades</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80aa-bb8b-eba664682dc8" class="">Previous: “How many bits can you export under leakage + correctness?”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-a821-fb0154dd6d02" class="">Upgrade v4: “How many <em>true bits about </em> can be extracted under stability + verification + certification relations?”</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8016-a2da-cf997cb371bf" class="">This is the only form that can legitimately claim cross-field relevance, because every field cares about , not raw .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8062-88fe-cba37006ac90"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e5-a0aa-d9ad045d9352" class="">6) Next move (max power): choose the flagship theorem class</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c6-b339-ca6dbd1beefa" class="">Pick one:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8079-8011-d8563d1c23e0" class="numbered-list" start="1"><li><strong>Truth separation</strong> (strongest conceptual impact)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8069-9f08-ccb4db7e1867" class="numbered-list" start="2"><li><strong>Phase transition in </strong> (strongest classification law)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ol type="1" id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8052-91dc-edaa19c8f038" class="numbered-list" start="3"><li><strong>Tight converse+achievability for </strong> (strongest operational theorem)</li></ol></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ee-b482-f21ba5ec205c" class="">If you answer “max power” again, the best first strike is (1): a clean truth-separation example that forces a new hierarchy.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-81d9-f43f4108eb24" class="">Upgrade v5: Make it “max power” by upgrading certification into a <strong>formal proof system</strong> (completeness/soundness/knowledge) and redefining capacity as <strong>verifiable knowledge rate</strong></h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8008-85bc-d2a84f3c1da6" class="">This is the first upgrade that is truly outside the usual framing while staying fully formal, because it replaces “truth” and “verification” with the same machinery used to define what it means to <em>know</em> something in cryptography and complexity theory.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8093-a16d-c0fb2e3acdab"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c6-a7e2-f463eeee3a63" class="">1) Replace “certification” with a proof system</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809f-ac0d-e9e635b7f5c6" class="">Let  be the truth object.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8016-8c32-daa1066d38a1" class="">Define a relation</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8012-9d17-d911c6952bf0" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\mathcal R(w,\pi)=1</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80de-8744-f78161cf145a" class="">A proof system is a verifier  such that:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a9-9ded-d3c8380e13cc" class="">Completeness</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808f-8cea-c985d4c0ecda" class="">If  is true (i.e., ), there exists  with:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80a4-945e-fad907f7fbc8" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pr[V(w,\pi)=1]\ge 1-\alpha.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-9366-d3e24dc3dd5e" class="">Soundness</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8019-bc3c-d39c545fa227" class="">If , then for all :</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8050-a6a9-eb1e9862d0e9" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pr[V(w,\pi)=1]\le \alpha.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-85f7-cc9beda68d99" class="">This replaces “semantic correctness” with a formal, adversarial correctness model.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-808d-9ed7-d561d9ab6ef0"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8021-a4d7-ee91c797a89d" class="">2) Replace “information” with <strong>verifiable knowledge</strong></h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8034-9bf3-ef17b7ea0fd5" class="">The key shift: information about  is not the objective.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80db-a7de-e07cc89b666e" class=""><strong>Knowledge about </strong> that survives proof verification is.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-806f-b026-ed24b674fbe0" class="">Definition: Verifiable Knowledge Extracted from an Artifact</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8026-8303-cd0ddfcf7dc7" class="">Let  be the exported artifact. Define:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e3-a306-dde67843820a" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathsf{VK}_T(W \leftarrow C)
:= \sup_{V\in \mathfrak V_T} I\big(W;\,V(C)\big).
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c6-b835-d8aa78d23f63" class="">This avoids the “identity verifier” collapse because  only outputs what it can validate under .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801c-bd65-c0ceab7f66c9"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8080-8430-cf045af46f69" class="">3) Add the missing axis: <strong>knowledge soundness</strong> (proof-of-knowledge)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8080-b695-e8a0ea1aaf6b" class="">Completeness/soundness are not enough for max power. You need a “knowledge” property:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8081-9eea-cf6145fa72b4" class="">A proof system is a proof of knowledge if any prover that makes  accept implies the existence of an extractor that can recover a witness.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-804b-854e-f7c1e802cc2a" class="">Formally (schematic):</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-801b-b6ce-f797cb4f4188" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\Pr[V(w,\pi)=1] \Rightarrow \exists \mathsf{Ext}: \mathsf{Ext}(w,\pi)\mapsto \text{witness}.</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-807d-9789-ca129dcc5051" class="">Now your artifacts can be treated as <strong>proof-carrying knowledge</strong>, not just claims.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c6-8e88-c7eb395add26"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b5-b764-df7618abe5ae" class="">4) New central capacity: <strong>Verifiable Knowledge Capacity (VKC)</strong></h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8028-b33e-cea5e47c337b" class="">Let  be your stability class (DP/RDP/etc.). Let  be verifiers bounded by time .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8074-871a-dae9190984fa" class="">Define the maximal verifiable knowledge rate:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8016-abfd-e41ac42a6483" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathsf{VKC}(\mathsf{Div},B,T,\alpha)
:=
\limsup_{n\to\infty}\ \sup_{\substack{M\in\mathfrak M(\mathsf{Div},B)\\ V\in\mathfrak V_T\\ \text{(complete/sound for }\mathcal R)}}
\frac{1}{n}\, I\big(W;\,V(M(X))\big).
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f1-afc4-d4ca0e259d7b" class="">This is the “Shannon object,” but for <strong>knowledge</strong> under:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8002-87fc-d39b4c18ac25" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">stability (privacy),</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d9-9975-f0fab2053b48" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">proof verification,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8029-bc3c-c7901e44e98a" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">adversarial soundness,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8042-9b61-c09c1cc01524" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">compute limits.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8070-80af-e407b3a64703" class="">Your previous “export entropy” capacity becomes a secondary quantity; the primary quantity is verifiable truth-rate.</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8035-8a37-d1450828e915"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-800d-81d5-f7e53dd743bb" class="">5) Rate region (max power formulation)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803a-b362-d18975eeb98d" class="">Define achievable quadruples:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809d-a645-fb43315d495d" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">(knowledge rate)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f5-8d27-c0a885c4ca53" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">(artifact rate)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8000-b6bf-fe3d0989b0eb" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">(stability spend)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-bb3c-ce423d59f280" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">(verification time class)</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8084-864f-e2adf1a647b2" class="">Achievable region:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80aa-a8f8-e66c0da43fe2" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathcal{R} := \{(R_K,R_C,S,T,\alpha)\ \text{achievable}\}.
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-802c-980c-ebf4d7fdc02b"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803c-85e1-c822b2483867" class="">6) Canonical max-power breakthrough theorems (now well-posed)</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ce-98c0-c69237dd2fa0" class="">These are the strongest possible theorem types in this upgraded calculus:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8040-ac01-c28bc08dfefd" class="">A) Knowledge separation (creates a new hierarchy)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8067-81ba-e7ce46d10cc8" class="">There exist families where:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-aa52-c02b243c4206" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
I(W;C)\ \text{large} \quad\text{but}\quad \mathsf{VK}_{\mathrm{poly}}(W\leftarrow C)=0.
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80f8-b592-e53b12a5cbe4" class="">B) Verification-compute separation (truth-rate version)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b3-90c4-f24e2eb40e8c" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathsf{VKC}(\cdot,\infty,\alpha)\ &gt;\ \mathsf{VKC}(\cdot,\mathrm{poly},\alpha).
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8094-8202-e88c32461bd8" class="">C) Proof-system phase transition (succinctness vs feasibility)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8042-82fa-d8fc0c174874" class="">Introduce a “proof succinctness” parameter (proof length, verifier time). Prove a sharp threshold:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8062-a659-d7771fd314c7" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">above it, ,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80b1-855a-ee79565f0e4f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">below it, ,<br/>under fixed stability budget.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h2 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80d1-92a4-e206a3a91f8e" class="">D) Tight converse + achievability (knowledge capacity theorem)</h2></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ed-b6e6-df57ee0d5190" class="">Find an explicit functional  and prove:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><pre id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8024-8561-d28c5fd03909" class="code code-wrap"><code class="language-latex" style="white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-all">
\boxed{
\mathsf{VKC}(\mathsf{Div},B,T,\alpha)=F(\mathsf{Div},B,T,\alpha,\theta(P_X)).
}</code></pre></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80c7-85b7-cfcc6b6ccb44"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1 id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8007-a4ba-f838706f06dd" class="">7) Why this is strictly stronger than all previous upgrades</h1></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8050-ae3c-e1449f9c5b95" class="">Previous upgrades: “verification” was a generic computation on .</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><p id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80e4-87dd-f49a50c687f9" class="">This upgrade:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80ad-aa62-fde9faec74a1" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">forces verification to be a <strong>sound proof system</strong> for a formal relation ,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-809d-8ab6-cbf604df7b06" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">turns outputs into <strong>proof-carrying knowledge</strong>,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8063-8c3b-da8548555819" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">defines the core capacity as <strong>verifiable knowledge rate</strong>,</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-80cb-9654-eede2390e734" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">makes compute separations and phase transitions mathematically natural.</li></ul></div><div field ultimately asks:</p></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><ul id="30ec5e6f-95bd-803e-a648-d5757cbccb8f" class="bulleted-list"><li style="list-style-type:disc">what can be regime.</li></ul></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><hr id="30ec5e6f-95bd-8082-9bef-de080f53b59e"/></div><div style="display:contents" dir="auto"><h1
